   if = { limit = { OR = {

    check_variable = { which = aqsc_known_modules_queued value >= 1024 }

   } } root = { set_variable = { which = aqsc_reason value = 5 }

    fleet = { is_upgrading = yes }

    check_variable = { which = aqsc_modules_queued value >= 1024 }
    check_variable = { which = aqsc_buildings_queued value >= 1024 }
   } } root = { set_variable = { which = aqsc_reason value = 3 }
 } }
   if = { limit = { OR = {
    NOT = { auto_qol_starbase_clear_normal = yes }
    NOT = { owner = { is_same_value = root } }
    NOT = { controller = { is_same_value = root } }
    NOT = { exists = fleet }
    fleet = { is_in_combat = yes }

log = "AQSC REMOVE_BEGIN version=0.2.5 category=modules"
if = { limit = { has_starbase_module = anchorage }
set_variable = { which = aqsc_remove_count value = 0 }
while = {
 limit = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_remove_count value < 1024 } count_starbase_modules = { type = anchorage include_being_constructed = no count > aqsc_remove_count } }
 change_variable = { which = aqsc_remove_count value = 1 }

}
while = { count = aqsc_remove_count
 if = { limit = { has_starbase_module = anchorage } remove_starbase_module = { module = anchorage } }
}
set_variable = { which = aqsc_type_after value = 0 }
while = {
 limit = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_type_after value < 1024 } count_starbase_modules = { type = anchorage include_being_constructed = no count > aqsc_type_after } }
 change_variable = { which = aqsc_type_after value = 1 }

}
root = { set_variable = { which = aqsc_last_before value = prev.aqsc_remove_count }
set_variable = { which = aqsc_last_after value = prev.aqsc_type_after }
log = "AQSC REMOVE_TYPE version=0.2.5 category=modules type=anchorage before=\\[This.aqsc_last_before] after=\\[This.aqsc_last_after]"
}
}

set_variable = { which = aqsc_type_after value = 0 }
while = {
 limit = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_type_after value < 1024 } count_starbase_modules = { type = arkship_system_scanner include_being_constructed = no count > aqsc_type_after } }
 change_variable = { which = aqsc_type_after value = 1 }

}
root = { set_variable = { which = aqsc_last_before value = prev.aqsc_remove_count }
set_variable = { which = aqsc_last_after value = prev.aqsc_type_after }
log = "AQSC REMOVE_TYPE version=0.2.5 category=modules type=arkship_system_scanner before=\\[This.aqsc_last_before] after=\\[This.aqsc_last_after]"
}
}

auto_qol_starbase_clear_execute = {
 remove_country_flag = aqsc_done
 auto_qol_starbase_clear_audit = yes
 if = { limit = { is_ai = no has_country_flag = aqsc_ready
  OR = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 1 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 2 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 3 } }
 }
 event_target:aqsc_selected = {
  if = { limit = { root = { OR = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 1 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 3 } } } } auto_qol_starbase_clear_remove_modules = yes }
  if = { limit = { root = { OR = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 2 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 3 } } } } auto_qol_starbase_clear_remove_buildings = yes }
 }
 auto_qol_starbase_clear_audit = yes
 if = { limit = { has_country_flag = aqsc_ready OR = {
  AND = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 1 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_modules value = 0 } }
  AND = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 2 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_buildings value = 0 } }
  AND = { check_variable = { which = aqsc_mode value = 3 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_modules value = 0 } check_variable = { which = aqsc_buildings value = 0 } }
 } } set_country_flag = aqsc_done }
 }
}
auto_qol_starbase_clear_reset = {
 remove_country_flag = aqsc_force
 every_owned_starbase = { remove_starbase_flag = aqsc_seen@root }
}

remove_country_flag = aqsc_slot_1_valid
 random_owned_starbase = {
  limit = { auto_qol_starbase_clear_normal = yes NOT = { has_starbase_flag = aqsc_seen@root } }

  save_event_target_as = aqsc_slot_1
  root = { set_country_flag = aqsc_slot_1_valid }
